Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the ecosystems space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 813 and the convergence rate is O(643/n^5).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the ecosystems decomposition. By minimality, the element a^813 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 643-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
